TYPE ArithmeticClock.CyclicCharacters.cyclicCharacter_orthogonality

  [inst : @NeZero.{0} Nat (@MulZeroClass.toZero.{0} Nat Nat.instMulZeroClass) N] (x y : ZMod N),
  @Eq.{1} Complex

                  (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Complex Complex.instNormedField))))))
        (@NormedAddCommGroup.toENormedAddCommMonoid.{0} Complex Complex.instNormedAddCommGroup))
      (@Finset.univ.{0} (ZMod N) (@ZMod.fintype N inst)) fun (a : ZMod N) =>
      @DFunLike.coe.{1, 1, 1}
        (@AddChar.{0, 0} (ZMod N)
          (@AddMonoidWithOne.toAddMonoid.{0} (ZMod N)
            (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{0} (ZMod N)
              (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{0} (ZMod N) (@CommRing.toRing.{0} (ZMod N) (ZMod.commRing N)))))
          Complex (@MonoidWithZero.toMonoid.{0} Complex (@Semiring.toMonoidWithZero.{0} Complex Complex.instSemiring)))
        (ZMod N) (fun (x : ZMod N) => Complex)
        (@AddChar.instFunLike.{0, 0} (ZMod N) Complex
          (@AddMonoidWithOne.toAddMonoid.{0} (ZMod N)
            (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{0} (ZMod N)
              (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{0} (ZMod N) (@CommRing.toRing.{0} (ZMod N) (ZMod.commRing N)))))
          (@MonoidWithZero.toMonoid.{0} Complex (@Semiring.toMonoidWithZero.{0} Complex Complex.instSemiring)))
        (@ArithmeticClock.CyclicCharacters.cyclicCharacter N inst a)
        (@HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (ZMod N) (ZMod N) (ZMod N)
          (@instHSub.{0} (ZMod N)
            (@SubNegMonoid.toSub.{0} (ZMod N)

                  (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{0} (ZMod N) (@CommRing.toRing.{0} (ZMod N) (ZMod.commRing N)))))))
          x y))
    (@ite.{1} Complex (@Eq.{1} (ZMod N) x y) (ZMod.decidableEq N x y)
      (@Nat.cast.{0} Complex
        (@AddMonoidWithOne.toNatCast.{0} Complex
          (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{0} Complex Complex.addGroupWithOne))
        N)
      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Complex 0 (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Complex Complex.instZero)))

TYPE ArithmeticClock.CyclicTwirl.conjugation

  @Eq.{1} Complex (@ArithmeticClock.CyclicTwirl.conjugation.{u_1} ι inst inst_1 N inst_2 w t A i j)

      (@DFunLike.coe.{1, 1, 1}
        (@AddChar.{0, 0} (ZMod N)
          (@AddMonoidWithOne.toAddMonoid.{0} (ZMod N)
            (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{0} (ZMod N)
              (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{0} (ZMod N) (@CommRing.toRing.{0} (ZMod N) (ZMod.commRing N)))))

          (@AddMonoidWithOne.toAddMonoid.{0} (ZMod N)
            (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{0} (ZMod N)
              (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{0} (ZMod N) (@CommRing.toRing.{0} (ZMod N) (ZMod.commRing N)))))
          (@MonoidWithZero.toMonoid.{0} Complex (@Semiring.toMonoidWithZero.{0} Complex Complex.instSemiring)))
        (@ArithmeticClock.CyclicCharacters.cyclicCharacter N inst_2

                    (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{0} (ZMod N) (@CommRing.toRing.{0} (ZMod N) (ZMod.commRing N)))))))
